```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, float)
    lo = -np.max(v) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - np.min(v) + 1.0
    # ensure sum at lo <= target <= sum at hi
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=np.clip(v+mid,0,1).sum()
        if s < target: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(v+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # fix tiny sum
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if diff !=0:
       # if capacity
       if diff>0:
          room=1-y; tot=room.sum(); y=np.clip(y+diff/tot*room,0,1)
       else:
          tot=y.sum(); y=np.clip(y+(diff/tot)*y,0,1)
    return y
```

    h0=h[p]
    m_min = n-1-p
    m_max = 2*n-2-p
    ms = np.arange(m_min, m_max+1)
    jf = p + ms - (n-1)
    jb = p - ms + (n-1)
    vf = (jf>=0)&(jf<n)
    vb = (jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))

    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]

B. LSE gradient implementation top-level.
```
def lse_weights(c, alpha):
    m = c.max()
    z = alpha*(c-m)
    z -= z.max() # already <=0; for numeric if m nan? no
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <=0: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; s=1
    else: w /= s
    return w, m
```
`weighted_grad(c? no need c, h, w, n)`:
```
g=np.zeros(n)
for idx, wt in enumerate(w):
    if wt < 1e-14: continue
    d = idx-(n-1)
    # first: p range [max(0,-d), min(n-1, n-1-d)] add wt*(1-h[p+d])
    lo = max(0, -d); hi = min(n-1, n-1-d)
    if lo <= hi:
        g[lo:hi+1] += wt * (1.0 - h[lo+d:hi+d+1])
    lo2 = max(0, d); hi2 = min(n-1, n-1+d)
    if lo2 <= hi2:
        g[lo2:hi2+1] -= wt * h[lo2-d:hi2-d+1]
return g
```
Check d positive: second p from d to n-1, h[p-d]=h[0:n-d]; yes. d negative: second p from 0 to n-1+d? lo2=max(0,d)=0, hi2=n-1+d; h[p-d]=h[p+|d|] length n-|d|; yes. For d=0 first adds 1-h, second -h -> 1-2h.
This computes sum over all weights; weights sum 1, so gradient of lse (without dx). Good.

Could optimize using if only top weights. We'll pass threshold `w_thresh=1e-12`.

val = dx*lse + mu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
w = exp(z)/sum; g = weighted_grad(h=x,w,n) * dx + 2mu*(x.sum()-n/2)

def add_smooth_noise(h, n, rng, sigma, decay):
    r = rng.randn(n)
    # FFT
    spec = np.fft.rfft(r)
    # lowpass: multiply by exp(- (freq/k)^2) maybe
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    spec *= np.exp(- (freq/0.15)**2)
    noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
    return project_box_sum(h + sigma*noise, target)
```

```
def trig_start(n, rng, harmonics=20, scale=0.5):
   x = np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
   f = np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,harmonics+1):
       a = rng.normal(0,1.0**2/m)
       ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       f += a*np.cos(m*np.pi*x + ph) # period 2? if m integer, cos(m*pi*x) period 2/m? over [0,2]
   f -= f.mean()
   h = 0.5 + (scale/np.max(np.abs(f))) * f
   return project_box_sum(h,n)
```

def feasible(L):
  interval = linear_interval
  if interval None: return False
  lo,hi=interval
  if has_diag:
    Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
    if 0 < Cq < 0.25:
       disc=1-4*Cq; srl=sqrt(disc); r1=(1-srl)/2; r2=(1+srl)/2
       # infeasible if hi < r1? Wait feasible if [lo,hi] intersects (-∞, r1] or [r2, ∞)
       # infeasible if lo > r1 and hi < r2
       if lo > r1 and hi < r2: return False
  return True
```

Final choose x:
```
def choose_x(lo,hi, Cq):
    # linear interval
    segs=[]
    if has_diag and Cq finite:
       if Cq < 0: return None
       if Cq >= 0.25: segs=[(lo,hi)]
       else:
          # compute roots (including 0/1 for Cq=0)
          r1,r2
          if lo <= r1: segs.append((lo,min(hi,r1)))
          if hi >= r2: segs.append((max(lo,r2),hi))
    else: segs=[(lo,hi)]
    # choose segment with midpoint closest to h0? Or maybe closest to old x to minimize movement.
    best=None; bestd=inf
    for a,b in segs:
       if b < a - 1e-12: continue
       mid=(a+b)/2
       d=min(abs(h0-a), abs(h0-b), abs(h0-mid))? Actually choose point in segment closest to h0:
          if h0 < a: x=a
          elif h0 > b: x=b
          else: x=h0
       ddist=abs(x-h0)
       if ddist < bestd -1e-15: best=x
    if best is None: best=midpoint overall
    return clip(best)
```

SLSQP wrapper:
```
def lse_objective(x,n,alpha): # no mu
   c=corr(x,n); m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max(); return float(m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z)))/alpha)
# gradient
def lse_gradient(x,n,alpha):
   c=corr; m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); s=w.sum(); w/=s; return weighted_corr_grad(x,w,n)
# constraint fun/jac
def constraint_fun(x,n): return float(x.sum()-n/2)
def constraint_jac(x,n): return np.ones(n)
```
Call:
```
res = minimize(lse_objective, h0, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_fun,'jac':constraint_jac,'args':(n,)}], jac=lse_gradient, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'disp':False})
```

Let's implement `weighted_grad_fft(h, weights, n)`:
```
bF = weights[::-1]
F = np.convolve(1.0 - h, bF, mode='full')
bG = weights
G = np.convolve(h, bG, mode='full')
grad = F[n-1:2*n-1] - G[n-1:2*n-1]
return grad
```
Need verify orientation and signs with small example.
Let n=2. h=[a,b]. weights for lags d=-1,0,1 correspond m=0,1,2: W=[w_{-1},w0,w1].
c_d:
d=0: a(1-a)+b(1-b)
d=1: a(1-b) (i=0 j=1)
d=-1: b(1-a) (i=1 j=0)
Weighted sum Q = w_{-1}b(1-a)+w0(a(1-a)+b(1-b))+w1 a(1-b)
Derivative wrt a:
terms: w_{-1}b(1-a): -w_{-1}b. w0 a(1-a): 1-2a. w1 a(1-b): (1-b). So grad_a = -w_{-1}b + (1-2a)w0 + w1(1-b).
Our formula: grad = F-G. F = conv(1-h, W[::-1]) segment. W[::-1]=[w1,w0,w_{-1}]. 1-h=[1-a,1-b]. Convolve full:
indices:
t0: (1-a)*w1
t1: (1-a)*w0 + (1-b)*w1
t2: (1-a)*w_{-1} + (1-b)*w0
t3: (1-b)*w_{-1}
F segment t=n-1=1 to 2n-2=2: [ (1-a)w0+(1-b)w1, (1-a)w_{-1}+(1-b)w0]
G: conv(h,W) with W=[w_-1,w0,w1], h=[a,b]:
t0: a w_-1
t1: a w0 + b w_-1
t2: a w1 + b w0
t3: b w1
G seg: [a w0 + b w_-1, a w1 + b w0]
Grad segment:
index p=0: (1-a)w0+(1-b)w1 - (a w0 + b w_-1) = w0(1-2a)+w1(1-b)-w_-1 b. correct.
p=1: (1-a)w_-1+(1-b)w0 - (a w1 + b w0)= w0(1-2b)+w_-1(1-a)-w1 a. Derivative wrt b: w_-1(1-a)+w0(1-2b)-w1 a. correct. Great.

Thus efficient gradient. Great.

```
def solve_coord(c, h, p, n):
    h0 = h[p]
    m_min = n - 1 - p
    m_max = 2*n - 2 - p
    ms = np.arange(m_min, m_max+1)
    jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
    jb = p - ms + (n - 1)
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms), dtype=float)
    if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vf? no jb]]
    cb = c[ms].copy()
    diag = (ms == n-1)
    has_diag = diag.any()
    if has_diag:
        idx = int(np.argmax(diag))
        a[idx] = 0.0
        cdiag = cb[idx]
    # unaff
    unaff = 0.0
    if m_min > 0: unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:m_min].max()))
    if m_max < 2*n-2: unaff = max(unaff, float(c[m_max+1:].max()))
    curmax = float(c.max())
    # if curmax <= unaff + tol? no affected max? But can still reduce.
    neg = a < -1e-14
    pos = a > 1e-14
    zero = ~(neg|pos)
    # If zero any cb > L check.
    Llo = unaff
    Lhi = curmax
    for _ in range(45):
       if Lhi - Llo <= 1e-9: break
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       lo=0.0; hi=1.0; ok=True
       if np.any(zero) and np.max(cb[zero]) > L + 1e-12: ok=False
       if ok and np.any(neg):
           lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L-cb[neg])/a[neg])))
       if ok and np.any(pos):
           hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L-cb[pos])/a[pos])))
       if lo > hi + 1e-12: ok=False
       if ok and has_diag:
           Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
           if Cq < -1e-12:
              ok=False
           elif Cq < 0.25: # including 0
              # If Cq very close 0/0.25? roots
              if Cq < 1e-15:
                 r1=0.0; r2=1.0
              elif Cq > 0.25 - 1e-15:
                 pass # all
              else:
                 disc = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
                 if disc < 0: disc=0
                 rt=np.sqrt(disc)
                 r1=0.5*(1.0-rt); r2=0.5*(1.0+rt)
                 if lo > r1 and hi < r2:
                    ok=False
       if ok: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    # final interval
    lo=0; hi=1
    if np.any(zero):
       if np.max(cb[zero]) > L + 1e-10:
           return h0
    if np.any(neg): lo=max(lo, float(np.max((L-cb[neg])/a[neg])))
    if np.any(pos): hi=min(hi, float(np.min((L-cb[pos])/a[pos])))
    lo=max(lo,0); hi=min(hi,1)
    if lo > hi + 1e-10: return h0
    # choose x
    if has_diag:
        Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
        if Cq < -1e-10: return h0
        if Cq < 1e-15:
            r1, r2 = 0.0, 1.0
        elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-15:
            disc=max(0,1-4*Cq); rt=sqrt; r1,r2...
            # segments
            best=None; bestd=1e30
            if lo <= r1 + 1e-14:
               segb=min(hi,r1)
               if segb >= lo - 1e-14:
                 x = clip_closest(h0, lo, segb)
                 ...
            if hi >= r2 - 1e-14:
               sega=max(lo,r2)
               if sega <= hi +1e-14:
                 x=...
            if best is None: return h0
            return best
        else:
            # all interval
            x=... maybe midpoint? choose closest to h0 in [lo,hi]
    # no diag or all feasible: choose closest to h0 in [lo,hi]
    if h0 < lo: return lo
    if h0 > hi: return hi
    return h0
```

```
def choose_feasible(lo, hi, h0, r1=None, r2=None):
    # candidate intervals:
    segs=[]
    if r1 is None:
       segs=[(lo,hi)]
    else:
       if lo <= r1:
           segs.append((lo, min(hi, r1)))
       if hi >= r2:
           segs.append((max(lo, r2), hi))
    best=None; best_dist=inf
    for a,b in segs:
       if b < a: continue
       if h0 < a: x=a
       elif h0 > b: x=b
       else: x=h0
       d=abs(x-h0)
       if d < best_dist:
          best=x; best_dist=d
    if best is None: # fallback midpoint of lo,hi clipped
       best=(lo+hi)/2
    return clip(best,0,1)
```

def coeff_coord(h,p,n):
    m_min = n-1-p; m_max=2*n-2-p
    ms = np.arange(m_min, m_max+1)
    jf = p + ms - (n-1)
    jb = p - ms + (n-1)
    a = np.zeros(n)
    vf = (jf >=0)&(jf<n); a[vf] += 1 - h[jf[vf]]
    vb = (jb >=0)&(jb<n); a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    a[p] = 0.0
    return ms, a
```

```
def slsqp_lse(h, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline):
    h0=project_box_sum(h, n/2.0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_sum,'jac':constraint_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_obj, h0, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=lse_grad, constraints=cons, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-11,'disp':False})
       if time... maybe can't interrupt; SLSQP runs uninterrupted. maxiter enough. But if n large and maxiter high, could exceed deadline. Set maxiter modest.
       nh=project_box_sum(res.x,n/2)
       return nh, compute_c5
    except Exception:
       return h0, compute_c5
```

```
def lse_obj(x,n,alpha):
   c=corr(x,n)
   m=float(c.max())
   z=alpha*(c-m)
   # clip z below -745 to avoid underflow? np.exp handles underflow to 0. But large alpha * difference maybe -1e12, exp underflow warning? Numpy may issue warning but not fatal. Use np.clip(z, -745, 0) to avoid.
   z=np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
   return float(m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z))) / alpha)
def lse_grad(x,n,alpha):
   c=corr(x,n)
   m=max
   z=alpha*(c-m)
   z=np.clip(z, -745, 0)
   w=np.exp(z); s=w.sum()
   if s==0: w[argmax]=1
   else w/=s
   return weighted_grad_conv(x,w,n)
```
Clipping to -745 keeps sum at least; underflow no warning. If many underflow to exp(-745)=5e-324 maybe nonzero? Fine. Could use -700.
Gradient weights normalized. If alpha huge, only max terms.

def exact_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None, tolerance=1e-9):
   target=n/2
   h=project_box_sum(h,target)
   c=corr(h)
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(c,n)
   for it in range(max_passes):
      if time.time()>deadline-? : break
      start_h=h.copy(); start_c=best? no current
      improved=False
      order=...
      for p in order:
         ms,a=coeff_coord(h,p,n)
         xn=solve_from_coeff(c,h,p,n,ms,a)
         if abs(xn-h[p])>1e-10:

             if c.max()*2/n < best_c - 1e-12? maybe update best? But sum infeasible. We can compute current unprojected c5; if lower than best, can keep? It violates sum. Not valid. ignore.

      if c5 < best_c:

      if not improved:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    lo = -float(np.max(v)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(v)) + 1.0
    # ensure target within possible; target n/2
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(v+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(v+(lo+hi)*0.5,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       if diff>0:
          room=1-y; tot=room.sum()
          if tot>0: y=np.clip(y+(diff/tot)*room,0,1)
       else:
          tot=y.sum()
          if tot>0: y=np.clip(y+(diff/tot)*y,0,1)
    return y
```
If v is all nan? no.

`corr_full(h)`:
```
def corr_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
```
`c5_value(c,n)`:
```
def c5_value(c,n): return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
```

`choose_feasible(lo,hi,x0,r1=-1,r2=-1)`:
```
def choose_feasible(lo, hi, x0, r1=None, r2=None):
    if r1 is None:
        if x0 < lo: return lo
        if x0 > hi: return hi
        return x0
    best=None; bd=1e30
    if lo <= r1:
        b = min(hi, r1)
        if b >= lo - 1e-12:
            x = lo if x0<lo else (b if x0>b else x0)
            if 0<=x? b maybe <0? clamp
            d=abs(x-x0); if d<bd: ...
    if hi >= r2:
        a=max(lo,r2)
        if a <= hi +1e-12:
            x = a if x0<a else (hi if x0>hi else x0)
            d=abs(x-x0); ...
    if best is None:
        best=(lo+hi)*0.5
    return float(np.clip(best,0.0,1.0))
```
Need if lo,hi outside [0,1]? We ensure. If r1,r2 maybe outside [0,1] for Cq close? We only call with roots in [0,1]. Good.

`coeff_coord(h,p,n)` as above. Use `n_float?` no.
```
def coeff_coord(h,p,n):
    m_min = n - 1 - p
    m_max = 2 * n - 2 - p
    ms = np.arange(m_min, m_max + 1, dtype=int)
    jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
    jb = p - ms + (n - 1)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    a[p] = 0.0
    return ms, a
```
`m_min` is int; for p=0 m_min=n-1; ok.
For p=0, ms length n; `a[p]=a[0]=0` diag. For p=n-1, a[n-1]=0.

`solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a)`:
```
def solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a):
    h0=h[p]
    cb=c[ms]
    cdiag=cb[p]
    m_min=ms[0]; m_max=ms[-1]
    unaff=0.0
    if m_min>0: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[:m_min].max()))
    if m_max<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[m_max+1:].max()))
    curmax=float(c.max())
    neg=a < -1e-14
    pos=a > 1e-14
    zero= ~(neg|pos)
    has_zero=np.any(zero)
    has_neg=np.any(neg)
    has_pos=np.any(pos)
    Llo=unaff
    Lhi=curmax
    for _ in range(45):
       if Lhi-Llo < 1e-9: break
       L=(Llo+Lhi)*0.5
       lo=0.0; hi=1.0; ok=True
       if has_zero and float(np.max(cb[zero])) > L + 1e-10:
           ok=False
       if ok and has_neg:
           lo=max(lo, float(np.max((L-cb[neg])/a[neg])))
       if ok and has_pos:
           hi=min(hi, float(np.min((L-cb[pos])/a[pos])))
       if lo > hi + 1e-10: ok=False
       if ok:
           Cq=L - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
           if Cq < -1e-10:
               ok=False
           elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-13:
               if Cq < 1e-13:
                   r1=0.0; r2=1.0
               else:
                   disc=1.0-4.0*Cq
                   if disc<0: disc=0.0
                   rt=float(np.sqrt(disc))
                   r1=0.5*(1.0-rt); r2=0.5*(1.0+rt)
               if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                   ok=False
       if ok: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    lo=0.0; hi=1.0
    if has_zero and float(np.max(cb[zero])) > L + 1e-8:
       return h0
    if has_neg: lo=max(lo, float(np.max((L-cb[neg])/a[neg])))
    if has_pos: hi=min(hi, float(np.min((L-cb[pos])/a[pos])))
    lo=max(lo,0.0); hi=min(hi,1.0)
    if lo > hi + 1e-8: return h0
    Cq=L - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
    if Cq < -1e-8:
       return h0
    if Cq < 1e-12:
       return choose_feasible(lo,hi,h0,0.0,1.0)
    if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
       disc=1.0-4.0*Cq
       if disc<0: disc=0.0
       rt=float(np.sqrt(disc))
       r1=0.5*(1-rt); r2=0.5*(1+rt)
       return choose_feasible(lo,hi,h0,r1,r2)
    return choose_feasible(lo,hi,h0,None,None)
```
In bisection, for Cq close 0.25 but less, roots close 0.5; ok. For Cq<0 due L lower than min, infeasible. But if Llo=unaff and unaff could be lower than minimum possible diagonal? If diagonal min > unaff, bisection will find L above min. Starting Llo=unaff may be infeasible, but ok.
Potential issue: For no affected diagonal? Every coordinate has diagonal its own c0. Cq at L maybe negative if L lower than cdiag - h0(1-h0). Bisection handles.
`curmax=float(c.max())` computed each coordinate; c changes as updates. Good. Could use `cmax` tracked but easier.

`exact_cd` as described. Need avoid `list(range(n))` memory? fine.
```
def exact_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng=None):
    target=0.5*n
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=corr_full(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_value(c,n)
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       h_start=h.copy()
       improved=False
       if rng is not None and it % 2 == 1:
           order=rng.permutation(n).astype(int)
       else:
           order=range(n)
       for p in order:
           if time.time() > deadline: break
           ms,a=coeff_coord(h,p,n)
           xn=solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a)
           xold=h[p]
           if abs(xn-xold)>1e-10:
               update_coord(c,h,p,xold,xn,n,ms,a)
               improved=True
       h_proj=project_box_sum(h,target)
       c_proj=corr_full(h_proj)
       cpj=c5_value(c_proj,n)
       if cpj <= best_c + 1e-8:
           h=h_proj; c=c_proj
       else:
           h=h_start; c=corr_full(h)
       if cpj < best_c:
           best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cpj
       if not improved or cpj > best_c + 1e-7:
           # if current was reverted and not improved, break
           if not improved:
               break
           # if worsened beyond tolerance, maybe try another pass? break to avoid cycling
           if cpj > best_c + 1e-7:
               h=best_h.copy(); c=corr_full(h)
               break
    h=project_box_sum(h,target); c=corr_full(h); cf=c5_value(c,n)
    if cf < best_c:
       best_h=h; best_c=cf
    return best_h,best_c
```

Now LSE functions with convolution gradient:
```
def lse_core(x,n,alpha):
    c=corr_full(x)
    m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s <= 0: ez[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; s=1.0
    lse=m + np.log(s)/alpha
    w=ez/s
    return lse,w
```

Weighted gradient:
```
def weighted_grad_conv(h, w, n):
    F = np.convolve(1.0 - h, w[::-1], mode='full')
    G = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')
    return F[n-1:2*n-1] - G[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check length: h length n, w length 2n-1. F length 3n-2. Segment n-1 to 2n-1 length n. Good.
Ensure w is float length 2n-1. If h contains values outside [0,1] during SLSQP, 1-h may negative; fine.

def slsqp_lse(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    h0=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
    c0=c5_value(corr_full(h0),n)
    try:
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for _ in range(n)] # cannot one-line? need list comp
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_sum,'jac':constraint_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
       res=minimize(lse_obj, h0, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=lse_grad, constraints=cons, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-11,'disp':False})
       nh=project_box_sum(res.x,0.5*n)
       nc=c5_value(corr_full(nh),n)
       return nh,nc
    except Exception:
       return h0,c0
```

`interp_h(h_old, n_old, n_new)`:
```
def interp_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new: return project_box_sum(h_old, 0.5*n_new)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=False)
    y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
    return project_box_sum(y,0.5*n_new)
```
`smooth_noise_start(h,n,rng,sigma,order)`:
```
def smooth_start(h,n,rng,sigma,bw):
    r=rng.randn(n)
    spec=np.fft.rfft(r)
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    spec *= np.exp(-(freq/bw)**2)
    noise=np.fft.irfft(spec,n)
    return project_box_sum(h + sigma*noise,0.5*n)
```

Now, custom projected gradient might be more budget-friendly. We can include if time, using LSE grad with alpha high and step. It can improve multiple coords. Let's implement simple `projected_grad_lse`:
```
def projected_grad_lse(h,n,alpha,iters,step,deadline):
   target=0.5*n; h=project_box_sum(h,target); best=h.copy(); best_c=c5; step0=step
   for it in range(iters):
      if time>deadline break
      val,w = lse_core(h,n,alpha)
      g=weighted_grad_conv(h,w,n)
      cand=project_box_sum(h - step*g, target)
      val2,_=lse_core(cand,n,alpha)
      if val2 < val - 1e-10:
          h=cand; step=min(0.2, step*1.1)
          if c5_value(corr_full(h),n) < best_c: best=...
      else:
          step*=0.5
          if step < 1e-6: break
   # final c5
   return best_h,best_c
```
But computing `lse_core` returns w; for candidate val2 we don't need w. `lse_obj` could be used. We can implement `lse_value(c?)` to avoid recompute? We'll just call. Need top-level `lse_value_core` returning value only.
This method can escape local? Subgradient with exact projection. Add if time after CD. But LSE value not C5; best tracked exact. Step based on lse. Could step too large because gradient magnitude? Let's set initial step 0.02. h changes by 0.02*grad; grad components maybe up to number weights*1 but weights sum 1, conv sums weights over lags; magnitude can be up to? For a variable, sum over lags weights influences up to n lags, but weights sum 1; each term coefficient ±value; gradient magnitude likely O(1). Step 0.05 ok.
However lse count value ~133; val2 difference smaller. Accept if `val2 < val - 1e-8`.
This might be cheap and helps.

`lbfgs_lse` function:
```
def lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    val, w = lse_core(x, n, alpha)
    diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    g = weighted_grad_conv(x, w, n) + 2.0 * mu * diff
    return val + mu * diff * diff, g
```

Let's draft code compactly.

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    lo = -float(np.max(v)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.clip(v+mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target: lo = mid
        else: hi = mid
    y = np.clip(v+0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - y.sum()
    if d > 1e-13:
        room = 1.0 - y
        t = room.sum()
        if t > 0: y = np.clip(y + (d/t)*room, 0.0, 1.0)
    elif d < -1e-13:
        t = y.sum()
        if t > 0: y = np.clip(y + (d/t)*y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y
...
```

`choose_feasible` minimal:
```
def choose_feasible(lo, hi, x0, r1=0.0, r2=1.0, forbidden=True):
    if not forbidden:
       return float(np.clip(x0 if lo<=x0<=hi else (lo if x0<lo else hi),0,1))
    best=None; bd=1e30
    if lo <= r1 + 1e-12:
       a=lo; b=min(hi,r1)
       if b >= a - 1e-12:
          x = a if x0<a else (b if x0>b else x0)
          d=abs(x-x0); ...
    if hi >= r2 - 1e-12:
       a=max(lo,r2); b=hi
       ...
    if best is None: best=0.5*(lo+hi)
    return float(np.clip(best,0,1))
```
We can always pass forbidden flag. In solve, if Cq>=0.25: choose_feasible(lo,hi,h0,forbidden=False). Else roots, choose_feasible(...,forbidden=True).

`solve_coord` code with masks. Need be careful modifying `neg[p]` when p index within size n. masks length n. Good.
```
def solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a):
   h0=h[p]
   cb=c[ms]
   cdiag=cb[p]
   m_min=ms[0]; m_max=ms[-1]
   unaff=0.0
   if m_min>0: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[:m_min].max()))
   if m_max<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[m_max+1:].max()))
   Llo=unaff; Lhi=float(c.max())
   neg=a < -1e-14; pos=a > 1e-14; zero=~(neg|pos)
   neg[p]=False; pos[p]=False; zero[p]=False
   hzero=np.any(zero); hneg=np.any(neg); hpos=np.any(pos)
   for _ in range(48):
      if Lhi-Llo < 1e-9: break
      L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
      ok=True; lo=0.0; hi=1.0
      if hzero and float(cb[zero].max()) > L+1e-10: ok=False
      if ok and hneg: lo=max(lo, float(np.max((L-cb[neg])/a[neg])))
      if ok and hpos: hi=min(hi, float(np.min((L-cb[pos])/a[pos])))
      if lo > hi + 1e-10: ok=False
      if ok:
         Cq=L-cdiag+h0-h0*h0
         if Cq < -1e-10: ok=False
         elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
            if Cq < 1e-12: qr1,qr2=0.0,1.0
            else:
               disc=1.0-4.0*Cq
               if disc<0: disc=0.0
               rt=float(np.sqrt(disc)); qr1=0.5*(1-rt); qr2=0.5*(1+rt)
            if lo > qr1 + 1e-12 and hi < qr2 - 1e-12: ok=False
      if ok: Lhi=L
      else: Llo=L
   L=Lhi
   lo=0.0; hi=1.0
   if hzero and float(cb[zero].max()) > L+1e-8: return h0
   if hneg: lo=max(lo, float(np.max((L-cb[neg])/a[neg])))
   if hpos: hi=min(hi, float(np.min((L-cb[pos])/a[pos])))
   lo=max(lo,0.0); hi=min(hi,1.0)
   if lo > hi + 1e-8: return h0
   Cq=L-cdiag+h0-h0*h0
   if Cq < -1e-8: return h0
   if Cq < 1e-12: return choose_feasible(lo,hi,h0,0.0,1.0,True)
   if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
      disc=max(0.0,1.0-4.0*Cq); rt=float(np.sqrt(disc))
      r1=0.5*(1-rt); r2=0.5*(1+rt)
      return choose_feasible(lo,hi,h0,r1,r2,True)
   return choose_feasible(lo,hi,h0,0.0,1.0,False)
```
Need `cb[zero].max()` if zero empty? We check hzero. But `cb[zero]` maybe empty? no.
When a has negative and pos, division fine. If a very small not in masks.

`update_coord`:
```
def update_coord(c,h,p,old,new,n,ms,a):
    d=new-old
    if abs(d) < 1e-14: return
    c[ms] += d*a
    c[ms[p]] -= d*d
```
Because a diag linear included. Good.

`exact_cd`: Use `np.empty`? We'll write concise.
```
def exact_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None):
    target=0.5*n
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=corr_full(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_value(c,n); bad=0
    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        h_start=h.copy(); improved=False
        order=rng.permutation(n) if (rng is not None and it%2) else range(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            ms,a=coeff_coord(h,p,n)
            xn=solve_coord(c,h,p,n,ms,a)
            xo=h[p]
            if abs(xn-xo)>1e-10:
                update_coord(c,h,p,xo,xn,n,ms,a)
                improved=True
        hp=project_box_sum(h,target)
        cp=corr_full(hp); cpv=c5_value(cp,n)
        if cpv <= best_c + 1e-8:
            h=hp; c=cp
        else:
            h=h_start; c=corr_full(h)
        if cpv < best_c:
            best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cpv; bad=0
        else:
            if not improved: break
            bad += 1
            if bad >= 3 or cpv > best_c + 1e-7:
                h=best_h.copy(); c=corr_full(h); break
    h=project_box_sum(h,target); c=corr_full(h); cf=c5_value(c,n)
    if cf < best_c:
        best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cf
    return best_h,best_c
```
If `rng.permutation(n)` returns array; iteration yields np.int64, okay. For it%2? use if rng is not None and (it % 2 == 1). Good.
`order=range(n)` if else; if p np.int64 in coeff arrays, okay.

Potential performance: `h_start=h.copy()` each pass; if break no. 700*100 copies small.

Now LSE:
```
def lse_core(x,n,alpha):
   c=corr_full(x)
   m=float(c.max())
   z=alpha*(c-m)
   np.clip(z,-700.0,0.0,out=z) # in-place? z new array; ok
   ez=np.exp(z)
   s=float(ez.sum())
   if s <= 0:
      ez[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; s=1.0
   w=ez/s
   return m + float(np.log(s))/alpha, w
```
`np.clip` no import? numpy.
`weighted_grad_conv`:
```
def weighted_grad_conv(h,w,n):
    f=np.convolve(1.0-h,w[::-1],mode='full')
    g=np.convolve(h,w,mode='full')
    return f[n-1:2*n-1]-g[n-1:2*n-1]
```
`lbfgs_obj` returns tuple:
```
def lbfgs_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
   val,w=lse_core(x,n,alpha)
   diff=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
   g=weighted_grad_conv(x,w,n)+2.0*mu*diff
   return val+mu*diff*diff, g
```
`lbfgs_lse`:
```
def lbfgs_lse(h,n,alpha,maxiter,mu):
   target=0.5*n
   h0=project_box_sum(h,target)
   try:
      bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
      res=minimize(lbfgs_obj,h0,args=(n,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=True,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-11,'maxls':30,'disp':False})
      np=project_box_sum(res.x,target) # variable name conflict np? use nh
      return nh,c5_value(corr_full(nh),n)
   except Exception:
      return h0,c5_value(corr_full(h0),n)
```
Variable `np` would shadow numpy! Use `nh`. Good.
`minimize` with `jac=True` and `bounds`: For L-BFGS-B, if `jac=True`, fun returns (f,g). Good. It may call with x as array.

No SLSQP.

Interpolation/noise/trig:
```
def interp_h(h,n,ntarget):
   if n==ntarget: return project_box_sum(h,0.5*ntarget)
   xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
   xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,ntarget,endpoint=False)
   return project_box_sum(np.interp(xn,xo,h),0.5*ntarget)

def noisy_start(h,n,rng,sigma,smooth=False,bw=0.15):
   noise=rng.randn(n)
   if smooth:
      spec=np.fft.rfft(noise)
      fr=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
      spec*=np.exp(-(fr/bw)**2)
      noise=np.fft.irfft(spec,n)
   return project_box_sum(h+sigma*noise,0.5*n)

def trig_start(n,rng,harmonics=18,scale=0.45):
   x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
   f=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,harmonics+1):
       f += (1.0/m)*np.cos(m*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
   f -= f.mean()
   mabs=float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
   if mabs>0: f = scale*f/mabs
   return project_box_sum(0.5+f,0.5*n)
```

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       par=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)

    except Exception:

    best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n; best_c=c5_value(corr_full(h),best_n)
    # phase 1 CD
    if time.time() < deadline:
       h1,c1=exact_cd(h,best_n,80,deadline,rng)
       if c1<best_c: best_h,best_n,best_c=h1,len(h1),c1
    # phase 2 L-BFGS-B
    n=best_n
    alphas=[]

       if time.time() > deadline - 120: break
       h2,c2=lbfgs_lse(best_h,n,alpha,mx,mu)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_h,best_n,best_c=h2,len(h2),c2; n=best_n
       h3,c3=exact_cd(best_h,n,8,deadline,rng)
       if c3 < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_h,best_n,best_c=h3,len(h3),c3; n=best_n
    # phase 3 multi-resolution
    base=best_n
    cands=[200,300,400,500,600,700,800,900,1000]
    # include relative, filter

    # insert base//2 and base*3//4? algorithmically
    rel=[max(40, base//3), max(40, base//2), max(40, int(base*0.7)), min(1200, int(base*1.2))]
    for x in rel: if x not in cands: cands.append(x)
    cands=sorted(set(cands))
    for nt in cands:
       if time.time() > deadline - 160: break

       ht,ct=exact_cd(ht,nt,30,deadline,rng)

          best_h,best_n,best_c=ht,nt,ct

             if c2 < best_c - 1e-12: best_h,best_n,best_c=h2,nt,c2
             h3,c3=exact_cd(best_h,nt,10,deadline,rng)
             if c3 < best_c - 1e-12: best_h,best_n,best_c=h3,nt,c3
    # phase 4 restarts at current best_n
    n=best_n
    for r in range(12):

       elif r % 3 == 1:
          ht=noisy_start(best_h,n,rng,sigma,smooth=True,bw=0.12 if r<6 else 0.25)
       else:
          ht=trig_start(n,rng,min(20, 12+r)) # literal? min with 20.
       ht,ct=exact_cd(ht,n,18,deadline,rng)
       if ct < best_c - 1e-11:
          best_h,best_n,best_c=ht,n,ct
          if time.time() < deadline - 80:
             h2,c2=lbfgs_lse(best_h,n,1000.0,30,500.0)
             if c2 < best_c - 1e-12: best_h,best_n,best_c=h2,n,c2
    # phase 5 final
    n=best_n
    h1,c1=exact_cd(best_h,n,100,deadline,rng)
    if c1 < best_c: best_h,best_n,best_c=h1,n,c1
    if time.time() < deadline - 30:

    h1,c1=exact_cd(best_h,n,60,deadline,rng)

    h=project_box_sum(best_h,0.5*best_n)
    c=compute? c5_value(corr_full(h),best_n)
    return h,c,best_n

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    lo = -float(np.max(v)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(v + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(v + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - y.sum()
    if d > 1e-13:
        t = (1.0 - y).sum()
        if t > 0: y = np.clip(y + (d / t) * (1.0 - y), 0.0, 1.0)
    elif d < -1e-13:
        t = y.sum()
        if t > 0: y = np.clip(y + (d / t) * y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y

def corr_full(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
def c5_value(c, n): return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))

def choose_feasible(lo, hi, x0, r1, r2, forb):
    if not forb:
        if x0 < lo: return float(lo)
        if x0 > hi: return float(hi)
        return float(x0)
    best = None; bd = 1e30
    if lo <= r1 + 1e-12:
        b = min(hi, r1)
        if b >= lo - 1e-12:
            x = (lo if x0 < lo else (b if x0 > b else x0))
            if abs(x - x0) < bd:
                best = x; bd = abs(x - x0)
    if hi >= r2 - 1e-12:
        a = max(lo, r2)
        if a <= hi + 1e-12:
            x = (a if x0 < a else (hi if x0 > hi else x0))
            if abs(x - x0) < bd:
                best = x; bd = abs(x - x0)
    if best is None:
        best = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    return float(np.clip(best, 0.0, 1.0))

def coeff_coord(h, p, n):
    m_min = n - 1 - p
    ms = np.arange(m_min, m_min + n, dtype=int)
    jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
    jb = p - ms + (n - 1)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    return ms, a
```
Note m_max = m_min+n-1 -> `m_min + n` as stop. For p=0,n=700, m_min=699, stop 1399 -> last1398. Good.

`solve_coord` as above.
```
def solve_coord(c, h, p, n, ms, a):
    h0 = h[p]
    cb = c[ms]
    cdiag = cb[p]
    m_min = int(ms[0]); m_max = int(ms[-1])
    unaff = 0.0
    if m_min > 0: unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:m_min].max()))
    if m_max < 2*n - 2: unaff = max(unaff, float(c[m_max+1:].max()))
    neg = a < -1e-14; pos = a > 1e-14; zero = ~(neg | pos)
    neg[p] = False; pos[p] = False; zero[p] = False
    zn = np.any(zero); nn = np.any(neg); pn = np.any(pos)
    Llo = unaff; Lhi = float(c.max())
    for _ in range(40):
        if Lhi - Llo < 1e-9: break
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        lo = 0.0; hi = 1.0; ok = True
        if zn and float(cb[zero].max()) > L + 1e-10: ok = False
        if ok and nn: lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - cb[neg]) / a[neg])))
        if ok and pn: hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - cb[pos]) / a[pos])))
        if lo > hi + 1e-10: ok = False
        if ok:
            Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
            if Cq < -1e-10:
                ok = False
            elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
                if Cq < 1e-12:
                    r1 = 0.0; r2 = 1.0
                else:
                    disc = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
                    if disc < 0: disc = 0.0
                    rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
                    r1 = 0.5*(1 - rt); r2 = 0.5*(1 + rt)
                if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                    ok = False
        if ok: Lhi = L
        else: Llo = L
    L = Lhi
    lo = 0.0; hi = 1.0
    if zn and float(cb[zero].max()) > L + 1e-8: return h0
    if nn: lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - cb[neg]) / a[neg])))
    if pn: hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - cb[pos]) / a[pos])))
    lo = max(lo, 0.0); hi = min(hi, 1.0)
    if lo > hi + 1e-8: return h0
    Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0*h0
    if Cq < -1e-8: return h0
    if Cq < 1e-12:
        return choose_feasible(lo, hi, h0, 0.0, 1.0, True)
    if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
        disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0*Cq)
        rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
        r1 = 0.5*(1 - rt); r2 = 0.5*(1 + rt)
        return choose_feasible(lo, hi, h0, r1, r2, True)
    return choose_feasible(lo, hi, h0, 0.0, 1.0, False)
```
Good.

LSE:
```
def lse_core(x, n, alpha):
    c = corr_full(x)
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0, out=z)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        ez[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    w = ez / s
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / alpha, w

def weighted_grad_conv(h, w, n):
    F = np.convolve(1.0 - h, w[::-1], mode='full')
    G = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')
    return F[n-1:2*n-1] - G[n-1:2*n-1]

def lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    val, w = lse_core(x, n, alpha)
    diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    g = weighted_grad_conv(x, w, n) + 2.0 * mu * diff
    return val + mu * diff * diff, g

def lbfgs_lse(h, n, alpha, maxiter, mu):
    h0 = project_box_sum(h, 0.5*n)
    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        res = minimize(lbfgs_obj, h0, args=(n, alpha, mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-11, 'maxls': 30, 'disp': False})
        nh = project_box_sum(res.x, 0.5*n)
        return nh, c5_value(corr_full(nh), n)
    except Exception:
        return h0, c5_value(corr_full(h0), n)
```
Could `minimize` with jac=True and args call `lbfgs_obj` with x as float? no.

Interp/noise/trig:
```
def interp_h(h, n, nt):
    if n == nt: return project_box_sum(h, 0.5*nt)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nt, endpoint=False)
    return project_box_sum(np.interp(xn, xo, h), 0.5*nt)

def noisy_start(h, n, rng, sigma, smooth, bw):
    no = rng.randn(n)
    if smooth:
        sp = np.fft.rfft(no)
        fr = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
        sp *= np.exp(-(fr / bw) ** 2)
        no = np.fft.irfft(sp, n)
    return project_box_sum(h + sigma * no, 0.5*n)

def trig_start(n, rng, harmonics, scale):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    f = np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1, harmonics + 1):
        f += (1.0 / m) * np.cos(m * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    f -= f.mean()
    am = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
    if am > 0.0:
        f = scale * f / am
    return project_box_sum(0.5 + f, 0.5*n)
```
`noisy_start` signature with bool; call.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)

        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)

Restart:
```

    for r in range(10):

       sigma=0.10*(0.65**r)+0.008

       elif r % 3 == 1:
          ht=noisy_start(best_h,n,rng,sigma, True, 0.15)
       else:
          ht=trig_start(n,rng,10+r,0.45)
       ht,ct=exact_cd(ht,n,15,deadline,rng)
       if ct < best_c: update
       elif ct <= best_c + 1e-3 and time.time() < deadline - 80:
           h2,c2=lbfgs_lse(ht,n,800.0,25,300.0)
           if c2 < best_c: update
```

Functions:
```
def periodic_lse_fg(base, m, reps, alpha, mu):
    n = m * reps
    h = np.tile(base, reps)
    val, w = lse_core(h, n, alpha)
    diff = float(base.sum() - 0.5*m)
    full_g = weighted_grad_conv(h, w, n)
    g = full_g.reshape(reps, m).sum(axis=0)
    return val + mu*diff*diff, g + 2.0*mu*diff
```
`minimize` with bounds [0,1] length m, jac=True. Then tile result.
```
def periodic_lbfgs(h_full, reps, alpha, maxiter, mu):
    n=h_full.size
    if n % reps !=0 or n < reps*10: return h_full,c5
    m=n//reps
    base=h_full.reshape(reps,m).mean(axis=0)
    target=0.5*m
    h0=project_box_sum(base,target)
    res=minimize(periodic_lse_fg,h0,args=(m,reps,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=[(0,1)]*m,jac=True,options=...)
    base=project_box_sum(res.x,target)
    h=np.tile(base,reps)
    return h,c5
```
This enforces sum base exactly via penalty; project. We can run for reps=5 from parent, different alphas. It reduces optimization dimension significantly. This may be the key.

Let's add `periodic_lbfgs` function. It reuses `periodize`? It uses base mean. At end tile.
```
def periodic_lbfgs(h_full, reps, alpha, maxiter, mu):
    n_h = int(h_full.size)
    if reps <= 1 or n_h < reps*10 or n_h % reps != 0:
        return h_full, c5_value(corr_full(h_full), n_h)
    m = n_h // reps
    base = h_full.reshape(reps, m).mean(axis=0)
    h0 = project_box_sum(base, 0.5*m)
    try:
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
       res=minimize(periodic_lse_fg,h0,args=(m,reps,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=True,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-11,'maxls':30,'disp':False})
       nb=project_box_sum(res.x,0.5*m)
       h=np.tile(nb,reps)
       return h,c5_value(corr_full(h),n_h)
    except Exception:
       h=np.tile(h0,reps)
       return h,c5_value(corr_full(h),n_h)
```
Need `periodic_lse_fg` before. It uses `lse_core` and `weighted_grad_conv`. Good.

We can combine full and periodic L-BFGS? `lbfgs_lse` for full is similar to `periodic_lse_fg` with reps=1. We can implement a generic `reps_lse_fg(base, m, reps, alpha, mu)`: if reps=1, h=base, m=n, base length n. But for full, base is h and mean target n/2. For periodic reps>1, base length n/reps. This one function can be used for both! Let's design:
```
def block_lse_fg(x, m, reps, alpha, mu):
    n = m * reps
    if reps == 1:
        h = x
    else:
        h = np.tile(x, reps)
    val,w = lse_core(h,n,alpha)
    target = 0.5*m
    diff = float(x.sum()-target)
    g_full = weighted_grad_conv(h,w,n)
    if reps == 1:
        g = g_full
    else:
        g = g_full.reshape(reps,m).sum(axis=0)
    return val+mu*diff*diff, g + 2.0*mu*diff
```
Then generic optimizer:
```
def block_lbfgs(h_full, reps, alpha, maxiter, mu):
    n_h=h_full.size
    if reps <=1:
       m=n_h; base=project_box_sum(h_full,0.5*m); tile=None
    else:
       if invalid return h_full,c5
       m=n_h//reps; base=h_full.reshape(reps,m).mean(axis=0); base=project_box_sum(base,0.5*m)
    res=minimize(block_lse_fg, base, args=(m,reps,alpha,mu), bounds=... length m)
    nb=project_box_sum(res.x,0.5*m)
    h = nb if reps==1 else np.tile(nb,reps)
    return h,c5
```
This replaces both full and periodic. Nice save lines. For full, no periodization after; it optimizes full h directly with penalty. For periodic, enforces. Great.

Now, L-BFGS generic:
```
def block_lse_fg(x, m, reps, alpha, mu):
    if reps == 1:
       h = x
    else:
       h = np.tile(x, reps)
    n = m * reps
    val, w = lse_core(h, n, alpha)
    diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * m)
    gf = weighted_grad_conv(h, w, n)
    if reps == 1:
       g = gf
    else:
       g = gf.reshape(reps, m).sum(axis=0)
    return val + mu * diff * diff, g + 2.0 * mu * diff
```
If reps=1 and h=x, `gf` length m. Good. If `x` is base, `x.sum()` okay.
`block_lbfgs`:
```
def block_lbfgs(h_full, reps, alpha, maxiter, mu):
    n_h = int(h_full.size)
    if reps <= 1:
       m = n_h
       base = project_box_sum(h_full, 0.5*m)
    else:
       if n_h < reps*10 or n_h % reps != 0:
          return h_full, c5_value(corr_full(h_full), n_h)
       m = n_h // reps
       base = h_full.reshape(reps, m).mean(axis=0)
       base = project_box_sum(base, 0.5*m)
    try:
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
       res=minimize(block_lse_fg, base, args=(m,reps,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-11,'maxls':30,'disp':False})
       nb=project_box_sum(res.x, 0.5*m)
       h = nb if reps == 1 else np.tile(nb, reps)
       return h, c5_value(corr_full(h), n_h)
    except Exception:
       h = base if reps == 1 else np.tile(base, reps)
       return h, c5_value(corr_full(h), n_h)
```
Note if reps=1, `h = nb` might be a view? project returns new. Good.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=... deadline=...
    rng=...
    try init
    best...
    h1,c1=exact_cd(h0,best_n,40,deadline,rng,1); update
    if best_n % 5 == 0:
       hp=periodize(best_h,5)
       hp,c=exact_cd(hp,best_n,60,deadline,rng,5); update
    # periodic LSE reps 5
    if best_n % 5 == 0:
       for alpha,mu,mx in LSE_SCHEDULE:
          if time>deadline-180: break
          h2,c2=block_lbfgs(best_h,5,alpha,mx,mu)
          if c2<best_c: update
          h3,c3=exact_cd(h2 if c2<=best_c+1e-4 else best_h, best_n,5,deadline,rng,5)
          if c3<best_c: update
    # full LSE reps 1, skip if little time
    for alpha,mu,mx in LSE_SCHEDULE[:3]:
       if time>deadline-150: break
       h2,c2=block_lbfgs(best_h,1,alpha,mx,mu)
       if c2<best_c: update
       h3,c3=exact_cd(h2 if c2<=best_c+1e-4 else best_h, best_n,5,deadline,rng,1)
       if c3<best_c: update
    # multi-res
    cand_list = CAND_NS + rel
    for nt in sorted(set(...)):
       if time>deadline-180: break
       period = 5 if nt%5==0 else 1
       ht=interp_h(best_h,best_n,nt)
       if period==5: ht=periodize(ht,5)
       ht,ct=exact_cd(ht,nt,25,deadline,rng,period)
       if ct<best_c: update
       if ct <= best_c+1e-3 and time<deadline-120:
          reps = 5 if nt%5==0 else 1
          h2,c2=block_lbfgs(ht,reps,500.0,35,200.0)
          if c2<=best_c+1e-4: cand=h2 else cand=ht
          if c2<best_c: update
          h3,c3=exact_cd(cand,nt,8,deadline,rng,period)
          if c3<best_c: update
    # restarts
    n=best_n
    for r in range(10):
      if time>deadline-90: break
      ... create ht full or periodic? Use best_h; if r%2 and n%5==0 periodize to enforce? Let's create ht candidate then exact_cd with period = 5 if (r%4<2 and n%5==0) else 1.
      if r%4==0: smooth noise sigma
      elif r%4==1: trig
      elif r%4==2: block flip
      else: high noise
      if period_use==5: ht=periodize(ht,5)

      if ct<best_c update
      elif ct<=best_c+1e-3 and time<deadline-80:

         if c2<best_c update
    # final

       if time<deadline-60:

          if c2<best_c update
    h1,c1=exact_cd(best_h,n,80,deadline,rng,1)

    h=project_box_sum(best_h,0.5*n)
    if best_n%5==0? don't periodize final unless it improves; leave best.
    return h,c5

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

LSE_SCHEDULE = ((20.0, 5.0, 80), (100.0, 50.0, 70), (500.0, 200.0, 60),
                (3000.0, 800.0, 45), (20000.0, 3000.0, 30))
CAND_NS = (200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900)

def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    lo = -float(np.max(v)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(v + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(v + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - y.sum()
    if d > 1e-13:
        t = (1.0 - y).sum()
        if t > 0.0:
            y = np.clip(y + (d / t) * (1.0 - y), 0.0, 1.0)
    elif d < -1e-13:
        t = y.sum()
        if t > 0.0:
            y = np.clip(y + (d / t) * y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y

def corr_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def c5_value(c, n):
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))

def choose_feasible(lo, hi, x0, r1, r2, forb):
    if not forb:
        if x0 < lo:
            return float(lo)
        if x0 > hi:
            return float(hi)
        return float(x0)
    best = None
    bd = 1e30
    if lo <= r1 + 1e-12:
        b = min(hi, r1)
        if b >= lo - 1e-12:
            x = (lo if x0 < lo else (b if x0 > b else x0))
            d = abs(x - x0)
            if d < bd:
                best = x
                bd = d
    if hi >= r2 - 1e-12:
        a = max(lo, r2)
        if a <= hi + 1e-12:
            x = (a if x0 < a else (hi if x0 > hi else x0))
            d = abs(x - x0)
            if d < bd:
                best = x
                bd = d
    if best is None:
        best = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    return float(np.clip(best, 0.0, 1.0))

def periodize(h, period):
    n = int(h.size)
    if period <= 1 or n < 10 * period or n % period != 0:
        return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
    b = h.reshape(period, n // period).mean(axis=0)
    return project_box_sum(np.tile(b, period), 0.5 * n)

def coeff_coord(h, p, n):
    m0 = n - 1 - p
    ms = np.arange(m0, m0 + n, dtype=int)
    jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
    jb = p - ms + (n - 1)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    return ms, a

def solve_coord(c, h, p, n, ms, a):
    h0 = h[p]
    cb = c[ms]
    cdiag = cb[p]
    m_min = int(ms[0])
    m_max = int(ms[-1])
    unaff = 0.0
    if m_min > 0:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:m_min].max()))
    if m_max < 2 * n - 2:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(c[m_max + 1:].max()))
    neg = a < -1e-14
    pos = a > 1e-14
    zero = ~(neg | pos)
    neg[p] = False
    pos[p] = False
    zero[p] = False
    zn = np.any(zero)
    nn = np.any(neg)
    pn = np.any(pos)
    zmax = float(np.max(cb[zero])) if zn else 0.0
    Llo = unaff
    Lhi = float(c.max())
    for _ in range(40):
        if Lhi - Llo < 1e-9:
            break
        L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        lo = 0.0
        hi = 1.0
        ok = True
        if zn and zmax > L + 1e-10:
            ok = False
        if ok and nn:
            lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - cb[neg]) / a[neg])))
        if ok and pn:
            hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - cb[pos]) / a[pos])))
        if lo > hi + 1e-10:
            ok = False
        if ok:
            Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0 * h0
            if Cq < -1e-10:
                ok = False
            elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
                if Cq < 1e-12:
                    r1 = 0.0
                    r2 = 1.0
                else:
                    disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
                    rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
                    r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
                    r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
                if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                    ok = False
        if ok:
            Lhi = L
        else:
            Llo = L
    L = Lhi
    lo = 0.0
    hi = 1.0
    if zn and zmax > L + 1e-8:
        return h0
    if nn:
        lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - cb[neg]) / a[neg])))
    if pn:
        hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - cb[pos]) / a[pos])))
    lo = max(lo, 0.0)
    hi = min(hi, 1.0)
    if lo > hi + 1e-8:
        return h0
    Cq = L - cdiag + h0 - h0 * h0
    if Cq < -1e-8:
        return h0
    if Cq < 1e-12:
        return choose_feasible(lo, hi, h0, 0.0, 1.0, True)
    if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-12:
        disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
        rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
        return choose_feasible(lo, hi, h0, r1, r2, True)
    return choose_feasible(lo, hi, h0, 0.0, 1.0, False)

def update_coord(c, h, p, old, new, n, ms, a):
    d = new - old
    if abs(d) < 1e-14:
        return
    c[ms] += d * a
    c[ms[p]] -= d * d

def exact_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng=None, period=1):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    if period > 1 and n % period == 0:
        h = periodize(h, period)
    c = corr_full(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_value(c, n)
    bad = 0
    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h_start = h.copy()
        improved = False
        if rng is not None and (it % 2 == 1):
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            order = range(n)
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            ms, a = coeff_coord(h, p, n)
            xn = solve_coord(c, h, p, n, ms, a)
            xo = h[p]
            if abs(xn - xo) > 1e-10:
                update_coord(c, h, p, xo, xn, n, ms, a)
                improved = True
        hp = project_box_sum(h, target)
        if period > 1 and n % period == 0:
            hp = periodize(hp, period)
        cp = corr_full(hp)
        cpv = c5_value(cp, n)
        if cpv <= best_c + 1e-8:
            h = hp
            c = cp
        else:
            h = h_start
            c = corr_full(h)
        if cpv < best_c:
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = cpv
            bad = 0
        else:
            if not improved:
                break
            bad += 1
            if bad >= 3 or cpv > best_c + 1e-7:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = corr_full(h)
                break
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    if period > 1 and n % period == 0:
        h = periodize(h, period)
    cf = c5_value(corr_full(h), n)
    if cf < best_c:
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_c = cf
    return best_h, best_c

def lse_core(x, n, alpha):
    c = corr_full(x)
    m = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0, out=z)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        ez[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    w = ez / s
    return m + float(np.log(s)) / alpha, w

def weighted_grad_conv(h, w, n):
    F = np.convolve(1.0 - h, w[::-1], mode='full')
    G = np.convolve(h, w, mode='full')
    return F[n - 1:2 * n - 1] - G[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

def block_lse_fg(x, m, reps, alpha, mu):
    if reps == 1:
        h = x
    else:
        h = np.tile(x, reps)
    n = m * reps
    val, w = lse_core(h, n, alpha)
    diff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * m)
    gf = weighted_grad_conv(h, w, n)
    if reps == 1:
        g = gf
    else:
        g = gf.reshape(reps, m).sum(axis=0)
    return val + mu * diff * diff, g + 2.0 * mu * diff

def block_lbfgs(h_full, reps, alpha, maxiter, mu):
    n_h = int(h_full.size)
    if reps <= 1:
        m = n_h
        base = project_box_sum(h_full, 0.5 * m)
    else:
        if n_h < 10 * reps or n_h % reps != 0:
            return h_full, c5_value(corr_full(h_full), n_h)
        m = n_h // reps
        base = h_full.reshape(reps, m).mean(axis=0)
        base = project_box_sum(base, 0.5 * m)
    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
        res = minimize(block_lse_fg, base, args=(m, reps, alpha, mu),
                       method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True,
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-11,
                                'gtol': 1e-9, 'disp': False})
        nb = project_box_sum(res.x, 0.5 * m)
        h = nb if reps == 1 else np.tile(nb, reps)
        return h, c5_value(corr_full(h), n_h)
    except Exception:
        h = base if reps == 1 else np.tile(base, reps)
        return h, c5_value(corr_full(h), n_h)

def interp_h(h, n, nt):
    if n == nt:
        return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * nt)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nt, endpoint=False)
    return project_box_sum(np.interp(xn, xo, h), 0.5 * nt)

def noisy_start(h, n, rng, sigma, smooth, bw):
    no = rng.randn(n)
    if smooth:
        sp = np.fft.rfft(no)
        fr = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
        sp *= np.exp(-(fr / bw) ** 2)
        no = np.fft.irfft(sp, n)
    return project_box_sum(h + sigma * no, 0.5 * n)

def trig_start(n, rng, harmonics, scale):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    f = np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1, harmonics + 1):
        f += (1.0 / m) * np.cos(m * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    f -= f.mean()
    am = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
    if am > 0.0:
        f = scale * f / am
    return project_box_sum(0.5 + f, 0.5 * n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        par = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n0 = int(par.size)
        h0 = project_box_sum(par, 0.5 * n0)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 450
        h0 = trig_start(n0, rng, 24, 0.45)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = c5_value(corr_full(h0), best_n)

    h1, c1 = exact_cd(h0, best_n, 40, deadline, rng, 1)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_h, best_n, best_c = h1, int(h1.size), c1

    if best_n % 5 == 0:
        hp = periodize(best_h, 5)
        hp, cp = exact_cd(hp, best_n, 60, deadline, rng, 5)
        if cp < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = hp, int(hp.size), cp

    if best_n % 5 == 0:
        for alpha, mu, mx in LSE_SCHEDULE:
            if time.time() > deadline - 180:
                break
            h2, c2 = block_lbfgs(best_h, 5, alpha, int(mx * 2), mu)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2
            cand = h2 if c2 <= best_c + 1e-4 else best_h
            h3, c3 = exact_cd(cand, best_n, 6, deadline, rng, 5)
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h3, int(h3.size), c3

    if best_n <= 900:
        for alpha, mu, mx in LSE_SCHEDULE[:3]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 140:
                break
            h2, c2 = block_lbfgs(best_h, 1, alpha, px, mu) if False else block_lbfgs(best_h, 1, alpha, mx, mu)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2
            cand = h2 if c2 <= best_c + 1e-4 else best_h
            h3, c3 = exact_cd(cand, best_n, 6, deadline, rng, 1)
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h3, int(h3.size), c3

    cand_list = list(CAND_NS)
    for val in (best_n // 3, best_n // 2, int(best_n * 0.7), int(best_n * 1.25)):
        if 40 <= val <= 900:
            cand_list.append(val)
    for nt in sorted(set(cand_list)):
        if nt == best_n:
            continue
        if time.time() > deadline - 180:
            break
        per = 5 if nt % 5 == 0 else 1
        ht = interp_h(best_h, best_n, nt)
        if per == 5:
            ht = periodize(ht, 5)
        ht, ct = exact_cd(ht, nt, 20, deadline, rng, per)
        if ct < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = ht, nt, ct
        if ct <= best_c + 1e-3 and time.time() < deadline - 120:
            h2, c2 = block_lbfgs(ht, per, 500.0, 35, 200.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2
            cand = h2 if c2 <= best_c + 1e-4 else ht
            h3, c3 = exact_cd(cand, nt, 8, deadline, rng, per)
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h3, int(h3.size), c3

    n = best_n
    for r in range(10):
        if time.time() > deadline - 90:
            break
        sigma = 0.10 * (0.65 ** r) + 0.008
        per_use = 5 if (n % 5 == 0 and (r % 2 == 0)) else 1
        if r % 4 == 0:
            ht = noisy_start(best_h, n, rng, sigma, True, 0.10 + 0.10 * (r % 3))
        elif r % 4 == 1:
            ht = trig_start(n, rng, 10 + (r % 9), 0.45)
        elif r % 4 == 2:
            ht = best_h.copy()
            L = max(5, n // 10)
            p = int(rng.randint(0, max(1, n - L)))
            ht[p:p + L] = 1.0 - ht[p:p + L]
            ht = project_box_sum(ht, 0.5 * n)
        else:
            ht = noisy_start(best_h, n, rng, sigma, False, 0.10)
        if per_use == 5:
            ht = periodize(ht, 5)
        ht, ct = exact_cd(ht, n, 12, deadline, rng, per_use)
        if ct < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = ht, n, ct
        elif ct <= best_c + 1e-3 and time.time() < deadline - 80:
            h2, c2 = block_lbfgs(ht, per_use, 1000.0, 25, 500.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, n, c2

    n = best_n
    if n % 5 == 0:
        h1, c1 = exact_cd(periodize(best_h, 5), n, 50, deadline, rng, 5)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = h1, n, c1
        if time.time() < deadline - 60:
            h2, c2 = block_lbfgs(best_h, 5, 20000.0, 40, 3000.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, n, c2

    h1, c1 = exact_cd(best_h, n, 70, deadline, rng, 1)
    if c1 < best_c:
        best_h, best_n, best_c = h1, n, c1

    h = project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
    c = c5_value(corr_full(h), best_n)
    return h, c, best_n
```
